       Overwrite or Cancel (proposal 33). Slice 10 adds the startxref
       window's hash and `content_hash`.
   - The old writers (`finish`, and incremental.rs's section) write
     through it, loads stop stripping `/Encrypt`, and encrypted files
     save encrypted by both old strategies.
   - **Deletes:**
     - lopdf's `save_to`/`compress` in `finish`;
     - incremental.rs's `write_object`/`write_dict`/`write_name`/`runs`
       (moved);
     - the `/Encrypt` strip in `load_pdf`/`load_pdf_mem`;
     - `write_atomic`;
     - the "encrypted: only Rewrite" restriction (docview.rs:1635,
       `WouldBreakSignatures.encrypted`).
   - **Checks:**
     - RC4-128, AES-128 and AES-256 files save encrypted by both
       strategies and reopen in PDFium with their password, keeping `/P`
       and `/ID[0]`.
     - An AES-256 xref-stream base whose strings live in object streams
       rewrites, and PDFium reads its `/T`, `/V` and outline titles.
     - A file with a 100-byte prefix appends and opens identically in
       PDFium, pdf.js and qpdf (ignored).
     - Reals round-trip at f64, and NaN is refused.
     - A file modified by another program between the save starting and
       the rename gives `FileChanged`, and nothing is overwritten.
     - Concurrent saves get distinct temp names.
     - Permissions are preserved.
     - `qpdf --check` passes on the outputs (ignored).
8. **Records** (XL; proposal 14; bugs 12, 15, 28 and 29).
   - **The new DocState tables and final keys:**
     `PageDef { base, copy, content }`, `Geom`, `PageAnnots`,
     `AnnotRec { Mark, Foreign, Widget { inst, merged, props } }`,
     `FieldNode` with `FormInst`, `FormDoc` with `ScriptState`,
     `Bookmark`, `OutlineRoots`, `Labels` and `Generator`. Today's
     header/footer becomes a `Generator` with legacy semantics.
   - **Liveness.** Records hold `BlobRef` and `Src`.
   - **Content.** OCR text layers become `OcrText` layers.
   - **Others' comments and links** become `Foreign` records.
   - **Template widgets** count in `Terminal.outside`.
   - **Open** builds the legacy header/footer `Generator` from
     `/SheafHeaderFooter`, without lexing. `/SheafGenerators` and
     `/SheafBlock` recognition arrive in slices 16–17.
   - **Attachments** become `Attachment` blobs in the Store: attach.rs:40
     (`store_blob`) and import.rs:391 intern the file, and
     attachments.rs:106, :117 (`blob_path`) read it through the mark's
     `BlobRef`.
   - **Indexes:** position, `/NM`, full name.
   - **The registry rule's destructures.**
   - **`compute`** is adapted mechanically. Its insert path gives each
     registration one `FormInst` (whose `src` is the registration's
     `SourceId` until slice 11) and keeps today's join for that
     registration's later runs.
   - **The legacy projection feeds the old writers.**
   - **`rebase_to_saved` is adapted to records,** for this slice only.
     After a save it re-points the cursor's `PageBase` to the saved
     file's `Src` by position, with copy 0, drops the OCR layers the
     file now holds, and rebinds provenance (`Mark.src`, `Foreign.src`,
     `FieldNode.src`, `Widget.src`, `Bookmark.src`) from the saved
     file's facts. It matches the way the app's re-read after a save
     matches today: marks by `/NM`, others' annotations by page and
     position, fields by full name, bookmarks by pre-order.
   - **Deletes:**
     - `removed_comments` and `saved_removed`;
     - `Doc.foreign_annots` and its `(SourceId, index)` keying;
     - `Document.forms` and `set_source_form`;
     - `fresh_annot_id`'s scan, `find_annot`'s scan, the linear
       `index_of`, and `page_positions` per call;
     - the DocState struct literals;
     - today's per-page widget map, and the `text_layers` and
       `header_footer` fields in DocState (they survive only in the
       projection's types);
     - the attachment cache paths (`store_blob` and `blob_path` in
       attach.rs, attachments.rs and import.rs);
     - slice 1's transitional keys.
   - **Checks:**
     - The model tests are ported.
     - The comments panel lists the same comments over the corpus.
     - Deleting another tool's comment and saving by both strategies
       leaves its string absent.
     - Duplicating a page whose comment was deleted doesn't bring the
       comment back.
     - M10 slice 4's merge and rename tests and M10 bugs 2–4 pass by
       both strategies, with `forms::check` clean.
     - `page_templates_stay` passes.
     - `a_files_pages_in_runs_join_one_form` and
       `corpus_a_form_combined_with_itself_is_two_forms` pass on records
       (one instance per registration).
     - A save, then an edit, then a second save by each old strategy
       keeps every mark, comment, field and bookmark (the adapted
       rebase).
     - A bench: 1,000 mark adds on the heavy 7k with no scan per mark.
     - The replay proptest passes on the new keys.
     - **Blob liveness:** add an attachment mark and a page stamp, drop
       every UI handle, then undo, redo, save and reopen; the bytes are
       intact.
9. **Rewrite from records; saves are outputs** (XL; proposals 15, 23,
   24 and 37; bugs 1, 3, 14, 15, 22, 23 and 29).
   - **`materialize/`:**
     - `plan` (Rewrite only, for now);
     - the emitters (`pages`, `content`, `annots`, `form`, `outline`,
       `catalog`), `copy`, `graph` and `verify`.
   - **Numbering and copying:**
     - stable `ObjKey` numbering;
     - raw passthrough under its five conditions;
     - ObjStm members re-serialized as plain objects;
     - Dense numbering for exports;
     - a balanced tree.
   - **Emission rules:**
     - inheritable keys written when a page is reparented;
     - the `/StructParents` rule;
     - merged field and widget pairs as one object;
     - the root ownership table.
   - **`keep_only` unchanged,** returning the ids it modified. Templates
     are kept.
   - **`rules::plan`,** with the new signature.
   - **Post-conditions:** reachability with templates, `forms::check`,
     copy-on-write and encryption.
   - **Saves become outputs:**
     - a `SaveRecord` synthesized at open and produced by every save;
     - `save_input` seals, and `record_save`/`abandon_save` unseal;
     - Save As is a Save;
     - **every save is a Rewrite,** for this slice only (slice 10 brings
       Append back). The tests that need an Append, meaning the Append
       halves of the both-strategies suites and M10 slice 5's
       signed-Append tests, are `#[ignore = "slice 10"]`;
     - recovery v2 in **Full** form, encrypted, with transforms applied,
       and the sidecar lifecycle;
     - signed files ask Rewrite or Cancel.
   - **Transitional:**
     - derived pages emitted as the original object with derived
       content, re-implemented in the emitter from the registration's
       `derived` record (save.rs's `origins`, `needed` and
       `copy_derived_page` aren't called);
     - the header/footer generator drawn on Rewrite;
     - OCR layers emitted in the legacy shape through
       `text_layer::install` (`/SheafOcrLayer`, `/SheafOcr`), which also
       strips a layer the base already holds. From slice 10, the old
       layer's streams are then unlinked, so the removal check turns a
       re-OCR over a saved layer into a Rewrite; that replaces
       incremental.rs's replaced-layer bails;
     - exports built by `subset` but written by this writer, with
       `rules::plan` and the `flatten_annots` post-pass.
   - **Deletes:**
     - the old rewrite path: `build_rewrite`, `rewrite_with`, `finish`,
       the `renumber_objects` use;
     - the old annotation and outline writers: `write_outline`,
       `write_page_annots`, `strip_sheaf_annots`, `link_connectors`
       (moved);
     - page and source plumbing: `LoadedSource`/`load_source` (moved
       into `graph`), `is_identity`, `reserve_pages`,
       `reserve_page_objects`, `copy_of`, and
       `origin`/`origins`/`needed`/`copy_derived_page`, whose only
       callers were the two old writers;
     - form rules: `rules::take_out` and forms/merge.rs's copy maps;
     - incremental.rs entirely, with its replaced-layer bails
       (incremental.rs:268, :322);
     - `rebase_to_saved` and its adaptation to records;
     - the legacy projection and its types;
     - the dirty tracking: `saved_outline`/`labels`/`annots`/`fields`,
       `baked_header_footer`, `removes_content`, `annots_dirty_pages`,
       `fields_dirty`, `header_footer_dirty`, `form_changed`, and the
       `Snapshot` dirty flags and sources map;
     - `save_to_path_noting` with its length-only disk check
       (save.rs:908–911), `Signed`, and `WouldBreakSignatures` (now
       `Ask::Signatures`);
     - docview's `Signed::Append` offer path (docview.rs:1635);
     - `/SheafPart` on written companions: a replacement's StrikeOut is
       recognized by `/IRT` and `/RT /Group` to a sheaf Caret (feature
       36), and `/SheafPart` is only read;
     - the app's reopen, re-hide and re-read after a save;
     - recovery v1, its temp primary and its leaked temp file;
     - the `geom_of`/`page_geom` wrappers.
   - **Checks:**
     - **Ports and identity:**
       - every save.rs rewrite test is ported;
       - after a corpus Rewrite, every surviving primary object keeps
         its number, and untouched ones are byte-identical;
       - the same input twice, and from two threads, gives
         byte-identical output.
     - **Outline, ownership and forms:**
       - outline `/A` URI actions, `/SE` and closed items survive a
         reorder plus save;
       - a duplicate moved before its original leaves copy 0 with the
         original object;
       - `forms::check` is clean on every corpus form after a
         page-delete Rewrite;
       - the M9 bug 14 reproductions pass.
     - **Emission rules:**
       - duplicating a page with a merged single-widget field gives one
         object per copy, with forms clean;
       - a page from a tagged file inserted into a tagged file has no
         `/StructParents`;
       - with an intermediate node carrying `/Rotate 90` and a
         `/CropBox`, a foreign page inserted next to it and a duplicate
         both keep their PDFium size and rotation;
       - owned keys survive set → save → undo → save → reopen;
       - `page_templates_stay` passes through the post-conditions;
       - a replace-text mark saves with no `/SheafPart`, and reopens as
         one mark whose StrikeOut is its companion; an older file's
         `/SheafPart` companions still open that way.
     - **Saves during edits:**
       - an edit and an OCR flush made during a slow save (a test hook)
         each land in a child version, stay dirty, and go into the next
         save;
       - a page delete on a hybrid form gives a sidecar with no XFA, and
         the export of a hybrid form that leaves out widget pages drops
         XFA.
     - **Tiles** aren't dropped by a save (an app counter).
     - **Gate:** a Rewrite at 7k takes at most 1.2× today's writer's
       time, with RSS at most 1.5×, on the synthetic and heavy fixtures.
10. **Append, the planner and the removal check** (XL; proposals 9, 23,
    32, 33 and 34; bugs 2, 13, 14 and 23).
    - **Appending:**
      - in-place Append with the journal;
      - `Onto::CopyOf` for Save As of signed documents;
      - the full `FileStamp` (slice 7's plus the startxref window's
        hash) and `content_hash`.
    - **The full planner:**
      - `classify` and `Effects` (§7.1), the planner being their first
        consumer;
      - path effects judged against saved values;
      - the `emit_deps` closure;
      - `present`, `BaseObjectsMissing`, `NoSaveRecord`,
        `TransformNeedsRewrite`;
      - Unchanged attaches the record.
    - **Signatures:**
      - `SigImpact` with allow masks, FieldMDP All/Include/Exclude, and
        `/Lock /P`;
      - "Append anyway", with the form transforms in the section;
      - UR3 kept within its TransformParams;
      - the Catalog `/Version` bump on Append.
    - **The page tree** is spliced in place, with node splits past
      max(128, size at open).
    - **The Append tests ignored in slice 9** are re-enabled.
    - **The removal check** runs on every Append:
      - the old side is read from the file on disk;
      - derived objects are judged on their inputs;
      - other objects are judged on decoded text;
      - a signed document asks.
    - **Recovery:**
      - Append sections with transforms;
      - recovered documents chained;
      - a restored document appends its `pending_tail` on its first
        save.
    - **Deletes:**
      - the always-Rewrite rule and Full-only recovery;
      - slice 9's "signed files ask Rewrite or Cancel": the signature
        ask now offers "Append anyway".
    - **Checks:**
      - **Removed text, every feature × both strategies:** the removed
        strings are absent from the decoded bytes (a page, a mark, a
        reply, another tool's comment, a bookmark, replaced mark text, a
        replaced OCR layer, and a re-OCR over a layer saved in an
        earlier session).
      - **Appends after earlier saves:**
        - a mark color change after a Rewrite appends one object under
          the same number;
        - a bookmark rename gives a Rewrite with the old title gone;
        - delete pages, save, undo, save, reopen: `qpdf --check` passes
          and the pages show;
        - undoing to the saved state gives Unchanged and a clean
          document.
      - **Durability:**
        - three consecutive Appends, then a reopen, pass `qpdf --check`;
        - a torn append (killed by a hook) is truncated on the next
          open.
      - **The Append ≡ Rewrite property** (32 cases, default run; over
        `Edit` generators until slice 11 ports it to `Op`), including
        labels i–iii with page 4 moved before page 1, links to pages
        added in later Appends, and a live-token reorder.
      - **The removal check:**
        - a forced misclassification is vetoed and logged;
        - the same veto on a P2 fixture asks and never rewrites.
      - **Recovery:**
        - the sidecar of an encrypted file with an unsaved header
          contains no plaintext;
        - a same-length sidecar against a changed tail is rejected;
        - a restored signed file's first save appends when its sections
          removed nothing, and asks with "Append anyway" when they did.
      - **Signatures:**
        - a P3-certified fixture appends an annotation silently, and a
          P2 fixture asks;
        - a signed file with a deleted comment offers "Append anyway"
          and leaves the signed bytes untouched;
        - a page insert into a signed **hybrid** form offers "Append
          anyway", the section drops `/XFA`, and the signed bytes stay
          intact;
        - a P2-certified, UR3-enabled fixture with `/Annots /Create`
          appends a comment with UR3 and DocMDP byte-intact, and no ask;
        - Save As of a signed file copy-appends, and its byte ranges
          still cover their bytes.
      - **Files:** a change by another program before an Append gives
        `FileChanged`.
      - **Gate:** one mark's incremental save on the 500 MB scan takes
        under 50 ms.
11. **Ops and Tx** (XL; proposals 20 and 25; bugs 8, 11, 12, 25, 27 and
    29).
    - **The `sheaf-edit` crate:** `Op`, `Transform`, the per-kind refs,
      `PageSel`, `Patch`, `NewMark`/`MarkPatch`, `Imported`, `BlobSel`,
      the specs, the envelope, schemars, `deny_unknown_fields`, and a
      custom ref deserializer.
    - **The types ops carry move into it** (§6.1): `PageId`,
      `OutlineId`, `AnnotId`, `FieldId`, `BlobId` (from store.rs) and
      `Local`, and every payload spec. sheaf-doc re-exports them.
    - **`Tx` and `ExportTx`.** `Document::lower_cx(actor)`; caps checked
      per resolved target before lowering; `Tx::check`.
    - **Lowering:**
      - lowering reads, write befores and decision reads recorded by
        value;
      - cascades to a fixed point;
      - typed `EditError`s;
      - locals and `created`;
      - host-owned attributes, with identity tokens literal for plugin
        actors;
      - describe and `Step.effects` per op;
      - `Notice`s;
      - progress and cancel.
    - **Inserts:** `InsertPages { pages: RangeSet, name }` with form
      instances. Positions resolve against the pre-op state.
    - **`Document::commit` at the cursor;** stale bases arrive in slice 12.
      `DocView::commit` returns `Result`.
    - **`SourceId = BlobId`.** Registrations go. Ops name sources with
      `BlobSel`, records hold `Src`, and `FormInst { src, copy }` is per
      op. A derived copy's `derived` record moves to a side table keyed
      by `SourceId`, until slice 17.
    - **Callers:** the app's call sites build Ops, and UI macros read
      through `Tx::reader`.
    - **Combine** is one Tx on the first file's document. It is still
      written and reopened, until slice 19.
    - **Job amends** go through a Tx.
    - **Transitional:** the WIT 0.2/0.3 → Op adapter, and an internal
      `SetPageBase`.
    - **Deletes:**
      - `Edit`, `compute`, `double_option` and `EditOutcome`;
      - `OutlineInsertTree` and `CommentsDelete`, and `OutlineMove`'s
        `with_pages` compute arm, re-expressed as `MoveBookmarks`
        lowering that also puts `Order`;
      - `cascade_delete`, `import_marks` and `merge_outline`;
      - the app's id-guess sites;
      - `sign_marks`, `keep_unseen` and `annot_from_wit`'s host filling;
      - `form_notice`, `script_sources`, `scripts_mixed`, `signed_from`
        and `next_copy`;
      - `take_in_fields`, `bring_fields` and `name_apart` as Document
        methods;
      - combine.rs's `insert_runs` and its one-version-per-mark flow;
      - the registration layer: `SourceId(u32)`, `add_source`,
        `register_source`, `unlogged_sources`, `next_source`,
        `Version.sources` and `Document::edge`'s source list;
      - describe.rs's 21-arm match.
    - **Checks:**
      - A serde round trip, plus a committed schemars snapshot that
        rejects additions without a default.
      - One test per cascade rule.
      - A batch with locals: a bookmark `$1` with three children, two
        connected new marks, and a reply to a new mark.
      - Unknown ids are errors in Move, Rotate, Delete, Duplicate and
        `ClearOcrText`.
      - The new-bookmark selection regression.
      - A `MoveBookmarks` with `with_pages` moves the pages its bookmark
        spans (`outline_move_with_pages`, ported).
      - sheaf-edit builds for wasm32-wasip2, and `cargo tree` shows no
        sheaf-doc under it.
      - A plugin update keeps `created`, `/NM` and tape rows.
      - The replay proptest and the Append ≡ Rewrite property run over
        `Op` generators.
      - **Forms:**
        - `a_files_pages_in_runs_join_one_form` (names `[a, a2, both]`,
          3 fields, 4 widgets);
        - `corpus_a_form_combined_with_itself_is_two_forms`;
        - `a_form_inserted_into_itself_goes_under_its_files_name`;
        - the notice-text tests (forms_saves.rs:306–336,
          forms_merge.rs:196–210).
      - Inserting `copy.pdf`, whose bytes equal `form.pdf`'s, names the
        parent `copy`.
      - `[delete-pages 1, rotate-pages {"pos":2}]` rotates the page now
        second.
      - Cap totality over (op, target kind) and over the actor table
        (§6.7), and a plugin actor deleting another tool's comment is
        `Refused`, as is `imported` from a plugin.
      - `Tx::check` on Delete with 7,000 pages selected takes under 1 ms
        and allocates no ids.
12. **One funnel and the history budget** (XL; proposals 13 and 19;
    bugs 9, 25 and 26).
    - **`Document::commit` with `Base` and
      `Stale::{Validate, Relower}`:**
      - value validation, and the post-merge cascade and uniqueness
        check;
      - recomputed lines and coalescing per key.
    - **ChangeSets** from every transition.
    - **Groups:**
      - `GroupKind::{Ocr, PluginCall}`, with amends that mint ids;
      - `GroupEnded` by ancestry, and interleaved fragments;
      - `revert_group` and History's "Undo all of …";
      - `rollback_group` that truncates or reverts.
    - **`DocView::commit` and `on_change`** are the only mutation and
      the only refresh.
    - **Background planners** commit with a base, concurrently with
      OCR's own run. `run_job` is one-at-a-time for file-writing jobs
      only.
    - **Dialogs** resolve to ids at open and re-lower their preview on
      each ChangeSet.
    - **`Snapshot`** is O(1).
    - **The plugin cell** (`ArcSwap`).
    - **The history budget:**
      - the byte budget prunes versions, with the detached saved anchor;
      - only saved and sealed versions are pinned;
      - older records are reduced to `SaveNote`s.
    - **Deletes:**
      - `DocView::apply`/`apply_batch`/`finish_edit`/`after_model_change`;
      - the hand-written `touched()` + `after_model_change` copies in
        `insert_file`, page_insert.rs and blank_review.rs;
      - `begin_job`/`apply_in_job`/`job_is_current`/`job_is_undone`/`Version.job`;
      - `reveal_new_bookmarks`' outline diff;
      - the global relayout per edit;
      - the `NoProgress` paths in page_insert.rs;
      - the transitional job-amend path;
      - `keeps_snapshot` for every version with saves, and "the root
        never passes the saved version".
    - **Checks:**
      - **The funnel:**
        - a `--script` run emits exactly one ChangeSet per transition;
        - a mark edit triggers no relayout;
        - `Snapshot` takes under 50 µs at 7k.
      - **Relower and Validate:**
        - an `insert_file` whose anchor page is deleted first gives an
          error notice and applies nothing;
        - an `insert_file` during a page move elsewhere is re-lowered,
          and both land;
        - a crop planned while the user deletes another page commits,
          and one planned while the user crops the same page conflicts;
        - a dialog based on V, then an OCR amend of V, then the dialog's
          commit keeps the amended layers;
        - a stale blank-review `DeletePages` against a concurrently
          added generator, bookmark and connector is re-lowered with the
          cascades applied;
        - a `Validate` commit whose cascades would add prims conflicts,
          and so does one that would leave two sheaf watermarks of the
          same z.
      - **Groups:**
        - undo during OCR stops the flushes;
        - OCR flushes interleaved with user edits fragment the group,
          and "Undo all" removes every OCR fragment while keeping the
          user's edit;
        - a plugin rollback with an interleaved user edit reverts only
          the plugin's versions;
        - a rollback after the plugin's version was saved keeps it, with
          a notice.
      - **The history budget:** 200 OCR versions on a 1,000-page scan
        stay within 256 MB, and a save after pruning past the saved
        version still appends correctly.
13. **Realizer, pool, render keys, view inputs** (XL; proposal 16; bugs
    22 and 24).
    - **Overlays:**
      - group overlays with slice 2's caps;
      - the per-source overlay allocator;

      - `RotatePolicy::KeepBase`;

    - **`EnginePool`:**
      - one worker;
      - visible engines pinned;
      - LRU by count and bytes;
      - a background budget.
    - **`RenderKey`,** with generators keyed on `needs()` and ViewInputs
      on what is drawn.
    - **`ViewInputs`,** including `content_only()`, and
      `Tx::read_render`.
    - **Background previews** for dialogs.
    - **Deletes:**
      - `RenderHandle`'s thread per source and `open(PathBuf)`, and
        `Doc.handles` per source;
      - `hide_annots`/`set_annots_hidden` and their replays, and
        docview's `drawn_marks` and `foreign_hidden` (hiding is data,
        `ViewInputs.hidden`);
      - the layers panel's decrypted temp copy;
      - the GPUI header/footer overlay;
      - `drop_stale_images` and the global `invalidate_tiles` calls.
    - **Checks:**
      - **Pixels and correctness:**
        - over a corpus sample, an overlay is pixel-identical
          (appearances only) to the saved file;
        - encrypted bases render;
        - a page rotated with an unsaved OCR layer renders once,
          rotated;
        - two edited pages in one group sharing a font, plus a
          duplicate, render correctly with no duplicate xref numbers;
        - an unsaved header is visible under view rotation.
      - **Invalidation:**
        - hiding a comment leaves print preview and the OCR input
          unchanged;
        - a 7k reorder re-renders nothing without live-token generators,
          and with "Page n of N" only the covered pages;
        - dragging a mark under `ByHost` changes no `RenderKey`.
      - **Engines and threads:**
        - no new temp files after using layers;
        - the thread count is constant across 20 inserts and crops;
        - engine memory stays under its cap after 200 groups;
        - background OCR doesn't evict visible engines.
      - **Gates:**
        - realize + open + render takes under 15 ms on the synthetic
          file, and at most 1.7× the warm base render on the heavy one;
        - after a crop and the legacy header on all 7,000 pages, the
          thumbnail strip fills at slice 2's rate within the cap.

16. **Watermark, background and strip** (L; proposals 30, 31 and 39;

    - **Dialog prefill** from the model.

    - **Unbacked blocks** are kept.
    - **Covered-but-empty markers.**
    - **The PDF version** is accumulated.
    - **A PDF/A or PDF/UA claim** gives a Note.

      - `watermark::install` and `read`, and the per-page ExtGState,

        (`StripLegacy` strips it);

        derive::watermark); Remove Header/Footer keeps `stamps::strip`
        until slice 17;

    - **Checks:**
      - **Resources:**
        - an image watermark on 7,000 pages adds one image object;
        - the object census shows one font, one ExtGState and one
          Resources dict per distinct base Resources.
      - **Text:**
        - "Café" renders;
        - CJK text draws `'?'`, with a notice.
      - **Removing others':**
        - Acrobat's reference watermark is removed, its Form XObject
          unlinked, and its text absent from the decoded bytes after a
          Rewrite;
        - a Word-made PDF's running headers survive a sheaf watermark,
          and `RemoveWatermark` reports them as authored and leaves them
          unless `include_authored`.
      - **Ours after reopen:** recognized, updatable and removable,
        including an image watermark (which rebinds), with `Scope::All`
        reaching a page inserted after reopen. A PDFium
        `FPDF_SaveAsCopy` re-save keeps our blocks recognized (ignored
        test).
      - **Saves:**
        - a rotate keeps the watermark upright;
        - adding a watermark to a saved file appends, and removing it
          gives a Rewrite with no trace left.
      - **Content safety:**
        - a stream shared by blank pages stays intact;
        - a front watermark over the bases `q …`, `Q q` and `Q` leaks no
          graphics state (a pixel check);
        - five save/reopen/watermark cycles keep the q depth at 2 or
          less;
        - `forms::check` is clean on watermarked form pages.
      - **Versions and claims:**
        - a PDF 1.3 base is written as 1.4 (Rewrite), or gets a Catalog
          `/Version` (Append);
        - a PDF/A-2b file gets the Note.
      - **Clean Copy** of a file with a legacy watermark has no
        `SheafSettings`.
      - **Thumbnails** fill at the gate rate after watermarking all
        7,000 pages.
17. **Header/footer and Bates; derived sources gone** (L; proposals 8
    and 31; bugs 16, 24, 28 and 29).
    - **The header/footer generator:**
      - live page tokens with `needs()`;
      - frozen Bates numbers persisted per block (`/N`);
      - frozen date and file;
      - `Scope::All`.
    - **Live redraws:** `LIVE_REDRAW` via `SaveRecord.drawn` (layout
      against recipe digests), and allowed drops in the removal check.
    - **`RemoveHeaderFooter`** by origin.
    - **Dialog prefill** from the model or a recognized generator.
    - **Then the last users of derived sources go.**
    - **Deletes:**
      - `stamps::install` and `to_catalog`/`from_catalog`;
      - derive::strip_stamps and `stamps::strip`, the old Remove
        Header/Footer (derive.rs:39, page_insert.rs:428);
      - `/SheafHeaderFooter` and `/SheafHF` (read only);
      - the legacy header/footer drawing and `Why::LegacyHeaderFooter`;
      - `SetPageBase` (header/footer strips were its last use) and the
        `RebasePages` remnant;
      - the `Derived` side record and `MemoryKind::Derived`;
      - `derived_copy` and `copy_form`;
      - derived-page emission in the emitter and the realizer;
      - sheaf-ops derive.rs;
      - page_insert.rs's derived flows (about 350 lines).
    - **Checks:**
      - **Live tokens:**
        - a reorder after a save renumbers "Page n of N", the save
          appends (`LIVE_REDRAW`), and the removal check passes;
        - an extract of an unsaved document keeps the binder's Bates
          numbers and renumbers page tokens.
      - **Frozen Bates:** Bates numbers don't change on a reorder, and
        survive a save plus reopen (rebuilt from `/N`).
      - **Applying:**
        - re-applying identical settings is a no-op;
        - with `Scope::All`, a page inserted later gets the header.
      - **Removal:**
        - changing the header text after a save gives a Rewrite with the
          old text absent;
        - a Word-made PDF's running heads are left by `SetHeaderFooter`
          and counted by `RemoveHeaderFooter`.
      - **Recovery** works with an unsaved header.
      - **No derived sources:**
        - a grep finds no derived-source code;
        - the forms-survive-page-tools tests (M10 slices 3–5) pass
          without copies.
      - **Speed:** a 7,000-page Bates applies in under 50 ms, with one
        font object.
      - **Gate, proposal 8's verdict:** after a save, reordering all
        pages of the heavy 7k under a "Page n of N" header gives an
        Append of at most 8 MB, planned and written in at most 1 s, and
        each visible page re-renders within the realize gate. The
        result goes in the slice notes as proposal 8's verdict; a miss
        means frozen numbers plus Update.
      - **Unencodable labels:** a label change that makes `<<label>>`
        unencodable draws `'?'` with a notice, and saving still works.
18. **OCR as content** (M; proposal 26; bug 14).
    - **The ops:** `SetOcrText` and `ClearOcrText`, in an Ocr group.
    - **Rendering:** OCR renders `content_only`, records the `PageDef`
      it rendered (`read_render`), and converts pixels with the
      render-time `Geom`.
    - **Flushes:** `Validate` per flush. A changed page's result is
      dropped and re-queued.
    - **Removing saved layers:** by the `/SheafBlock` stream marker, or
      by the `/ShOCR` marker inside merged streams. A legacy
      `/SheafOcrLayer` is removed together with its leading `q`.
    - **Re-OCR** replaces a saved layer.
    - **Deletes:**
      - `text_layer::install`, `strip` and `has_layer`, and with them
        slice 9's legacy-shape OCR emission;
      - the presence-means-unsaved remnants;
      - the plugin API's OCR overrides;
      - `/SheafOcr` as the OCR font's resource name, and
        `/SheafOcrLayer` as a written marker (new layers use `/Sh…`
        names and `/SheafBlock`).
    - **Checks:**
      - **Finding and saving:**
        - OCR, then Find, hits words before saving;
        - OCR plus an incremental save appends one stream per page, with
          the glyphless font once per file;
        - an empty layer writes nothing.
      - **Removing saved layers:**
        - replacing a saved layer gives a Rewrite with the old words
          absent;
        - `ClearOcrText` removes a saved layer, including one inside
          streams another tool merged;
        - removing a legacy layer leaves q/Q balanced.
      - **During a run:**
        - a rotate or crop during a run doesn't conflict, and the words
          land correctly;
        - a `ReplacePages` drops that page's result, which is re-queued;
        - OCR of a page with an unsaved header doesn't record the
          header's text.
      - **Undo:** an OCR run is one undo step, and undo during it stops
        the flushes.

      helpers.

      - **Staleness:**
        - the subtotal scenario (tapes on page 3 read, the user edits a
          tape, the plugin adds a total on page 5) conflicts;
        - a user edit to an unrelated page during a call is re-lowered,
          and both land.
      - **Caps:** `InsertPages` and deleting another tool's comment are
        `Refused`.
      - **Privacy:** the plugin never receives the user's name (authored
        marks report a role, `<<name>>` stays literal).
      - **Events:**
        - a plugin isn't sent its own changes;
        - depth-2 echoes stop;
        - an undo-fighting fixture's edits after an undo are refused
          with `edit-refused`;
        - coalesced events carry the union of pages.
      - **Versions:**
        - amount-crossref refuses cached ids after a reopen;
        - a plugin declaring a newer `edit-schema` minor is refused at
          load.
      - **Assets:** an uploaded asset becomes a page-stamp mark.
      - **Build:** exactly one bindgen invocation remains.

## M10 slices 6+ on the new API

M10 resumes on `main` after **all** of M10.5 (slices 1–20; proposal 11).
Slice 20 rewrites `m10.md`'s slices to match this list.

- **6, text appearances.** A pure
  `appearance::text(field, widget, display, &FormDoc, font) -> FormRes`
  on ContentWriter and `Font`. It covers the `/DA` parser; WinAnsi,
  MacRoman and `/Differences` encoders as `Font`s; `/DR` handles from
  `SourceForm`; `/MK /R`; `WidgetProps.border`; and `/Tx BMC` with a
  clip. The emitter calls it for widgets whose appearance digest
  changed, and so does the realizer, so the screen equals the file. The
  overlay-or-pictures spike is settled.
- **7, the Unicode fallback.** `Res::Font(Fallback)`: a GID-preserving
  Noto Type0 subset, late-bound per materialization or overlay. An
  Append adds a new subset object when new glyphs appear. NFC, MaxLen by
  grapheme and the refusal live in `SetFieldValues`' lowering. It ends
  the `'?'` interim in marks and generators.
- **8, buttons, radios and choices.** Pure generators. Group `/AS`
  semantics are lowering rules of `SetFieldValues`.
- **9, saving values. It shrinks to one commit.**
  `SetFieldValues { values (by FieldId or full name), display, calculated }`
  lowers to `Put Field`/`Put Annot`. Both strategies, recovery and
  identity after a save already exist, so 9a's re-binding is deleted.
  The save rules are not per-writer code:
  - a replaced value is REPLACES_TEXT, so a Rewrite;
  - filling an empty field appends, keeping UR3 within its
    TransformParams;
  - earlier-revision values are a `SourceForm` fact that forces a
    Rewrite on the first form change;
  - XFA (tooltips to `/TU`, after `XfaHoldsValues`), `/UR3` and SigFlags
    are `rules::plan` transforms;
  - signed and certified files go through §9.3's asks, with "Append
    anyway";
  - lowering refuses edits to fields locked by a known signature
    (FieldMDP), and to signed fields themselves, with the
    `EditError::Locked { field }` this slice adds;
  - the encryption notice is gone, because encryption is kept.

  Check: a fill on a signed hybrid fixture offers "Append anyway" and
  doesn't rewrite unasked.
- **10, focus, keys and Tab order.** Hit tests on PageSpace widgets in
  `page_annots` order. **It adds `KeyPatch` with symbolic `PatchValue`s,
  and `PageDef.keys` for `/Tabs`, with a classify role row (`/Tabs` →
  FIELD_TREE, OTHER).** The `/S` order comes from kept.rs's structure
  walker over source facts. Pending UI commits flush before any plugin
  call.
- **11, the field editor. It adds `GroupKind::Typing`:** one group per
  focus session, each commit a `SetFieldValues` amend. The editor's text
  stays uncommitted, with its own Ctrl+Z. `ViewInputs` hides the focused
  widget from the realization while the editor draws.
- **12, drawing changed fields.** It becomes "marks drawn by PDFium":
  switch to `MarkDraw::ByEngine`; delete the GPUI mark painter
  (`annot_elements`), `stamp_preview.rs` and the tape pictures; and
  decide FFLDraw (recommended: appearances only, once every changed
  field has an `/AP`).
- **13, buttons and Clear Form.** `ResetFields` as one Tx. Hide is
  `PatchWidgets { flags }`.
- **14, the Fields panel, and values in search.** Model reads;
  `TextOpts { fields: true }` merges widget display text, for the app.
- **15, flatten.** `FlattenFields` shares `FlattenAnnotations`'
  machinery:
  - ensure appearances;
  - draw printable widgets by `/AS`, with no first-state fallback, into
    a `Placements` layer of `FormRef::Pinned`;
  - remove widgets through `widget.field`;
  - empty the `FormDoc`;
  - pass `removed_annots` to `keep_only`.

  **It adds `FormBring::Flatten`** for Insert and Combine, and
  `SignatureNotValidHere` fields may flatten there. Also
  `sheaf form flatten`.
- **16–17, form data.** FDF, XFDF, CSV, text and JSON importers produce
  `SetFieldValues` batches by name, resolved through calculation and
  committed `Validate`. Exporters are queries.
- **18, AF functions.** Pure Rust, in the host's resolve stage. They
  produce display and calculated values plus their decision reads.
- **19–22, scripts. They add `Actor::FormScript` and
  `PageSource::Template`** (spawning pages from templates).
  `FormsHost::resolve(&Snapshot, Vec<Op>) -> (Vec<Op>, reads)` runs on
  the formjs worker, then commits `Validate` into the user's Typing
  group. The sandbox syncs on `ChangeSet.fields` for transitions not
  made by `FormScript`. Replay never runs scripts, and a save waits for
  a running cascade's group.
- **23–26, Prepare Form.** `AddField`, `AddWidget`, `PatchFields`,
  `PatchWidgets`, `MoveWidgets`, `DeleteFields`, `DeleteWidgets`,
  `RenameField`, `MoveField`, `SetTabOrder` and `SetCalculationOrder`,
  over existing keys. **They add `FieldNode.patch`, widget patches
  (`PatchValue`) and role rows** (removing `/AA` or `/JS` → REMOVES;
  `/AP` on a widget → FILL). Multi-select is one Tx with locals.
  Deleting is REMOVES, so a Rewrite. Copies are untagged until M14.
- **27–28, detection.** Queries over the TextService and the pool.
  Accepting the proposals is one Tx.
- **29, the plugin API.** Field ops join the JSON `Op` as `Cap::Plugin`,
  with no WIT bump. Field reads are new WIT functions in
  `sheaf:plugin@0.5.0`, behind a manifest `fields` capability that also
  unlocks widgets in `annotations()`, `TextOpts { fields }` and
  `FormDraw::Appearances` for renders. Without it, plugin renders use
  `FormDraw::Redacted`.

**Edits to `m10.md`'s proposals and slices** (slice 20):
- proposal 8: delete its "Re-bound after a save" bullet and the dirty
  tracking of saved values;
- proposal 10: replace the on-screen slot-hiding bullet with
  `ViewInputs`;
- proposal 7's "every way out": point it at `rules::plan` plus the
  export preflight;
- proposal 18: becomes `FlattenFields`/`FlattenAnnotations`;
- proposal 23: renumbered to 0.5, with no 0.3 or 0.4 serving;
- slice 9: drop 9a's re-binding;
- slice 11: the Typing group;
- slice 12: "marks drawn by PDFium";
- slice 15: `PinnedAp`;
- slice 29: 0.5 with `fields`, with no 0.3 or 0.4 serving; `m11.md`'s
  plugin API becomes 0.6; `docs/forms.md` drops "encryption until M11"
  from the save rules (m10.md:1817–1834).

    splice, `Balance`, TextBag; §13), adds `ContentStack.rewrite` and
    `Static::Ops`, and lowers redaction as definitions evaluated at emit
    (§20).
  - **The leak check** (its proposal 8) runs over the output, not
    DocState (§20).
  - **Applying redactions** is a `Transform` on an `ExportTx`, so
    plugins can't apply them by type; "every way out asks first" is the

  - **Its plugin API bump** (proposal 15) becomes 0.6, since M10 takes
    0.5.
  - **Its proposal 11** (no plaintext copies on disk) is mostly met
    here: generated sources are memory blobs, and the sidecar is
    encrypted.
  - **New AES-256 encryption** (V5, R6) writes `/Length 256` in
    `/Encrypt`, in M11's encryption code or as a `vendor/lopdf` patch:
    lopdf's `Document::encrypt` leaves it out, and poppler 26.01 then
    garbles every stream. Its tests check new AES-256 output with
    poppler (M10.5 slice 2's notes).
- **[`agent.md`](agent.md):**
  - "Submit edits" (agent.md:129): sheaf-edit `Op` JSON in a versioned
    envelope, through `Document::plan(actor, base, ops) -> PlanHandle`
    and `commit(plan)`, so the approval card commits exactly what it
    showed (§20).
  - "Versions keep reads and writes honest" (:133–136): version tokens
    are `{ instance, version }`; the agent's reads are decision reads
    kept per MCP session, and it commits `Validate`, so a user edit
    elsewhere doesn't refuse it and it can write during OCR; plans
    expire on conflict.
  - The History row (:127): `VersionView` with stored lines, not
    `describe.rs` at read time; `Document::edge`'s source list is gone.
  - The author "Claude for GE" (:166–168): `Actor::Agent` is session
    provenance; nothing goes into the file except a mark's `/T`, which
    the host sets.
  - "Revert this turn" (:169–171): a revert of the turn's versions; a
    jump only when nothing came after the turn.
  - Its own transport and approval UI, not the sheaf-instance socket
    (proposal 36); semantic ops only at first (decision 6); field values
    behind its per-document consent.
- **[`file-contention.md`](file-contention.md):**
  - §3 Merge (file-contention.md:147–163): a version's steps now hold
    resolved ops and prims with before-values, so a merge applies them
    onto the other person's version by value (`Validate`) or re-lowers
    the resolved ops (`Relower`), and conflicts are explicit. Marks
    still map by `/NM`. Sheaf marks' `/NM` embeds the `AnnotId`, so it
    is unique per document (duplicates get fresh ids); others' `/NM` is
    unique per page only. `Document::edge`'s source list is gone, since
    sources are content-addressed.
  - "Someone else saved" is detected by the `FileStamp` re-check before
    every write, which asks Save As…, Overwrite or Cancel (proposal 33).

  - [`plugins.md`](plugins.md)'s "the host keeps N-1 worlds" (:108,

## Risks

- **Scope.** The plan is 20 slices, about 68 points, with M10 paused
  throughout.
  - *Mitigations:* slices 1–4 bring value on their own (exact replay,
    measured risks, the Store, one open). M10's slices 6–9 shrink by
    about one slice (the spike, the re-binding and the per-writer save
    rules go).
- **The lazy reader must be exact** on damaged, hybrid, encrypted and
  hostile files.
  - *Mitigations:* corpus-wide lazy-against-eager equality, the eager
    fallback, fuzzing and one `Limits`.
- **Overlays on real files are unmeasured.** That covers PDFium with
  in-memory append sections on encrypted, xref-stream, hybrid,
  linearized, damaged and leading-junk bases, and widgets after a page
  is replaced in place.
  - *Mitigation:* slice 2's written criteria, with fallbacks (a
    decrypted image per source, standalone groups) decided before any
    slice depends on them.
- **The change to f64 reals** touches about 56 sites and may shift
  output formatting.
  - *Mitigation:* the save suites and a corpus round trip in slice 3.
- **The legacy projection and the adapted `rebase_to_saved`** (slice 8)
  feed the old writers while records exist.
  - *Mitigations:* they live for one slice only and cross no coordinate
    or appearance change; the replay proptest and every save suite run
    through them; both are deleted in slice 9.
- **No-rebase saves and in-place Appends** depend on the `SaveRecord`
  (`present`, owned roots, `tree`, stamps) being right across
  alternating Append, Rewrite, Save As and recovery. A mistake writes a
  broken file.
  - *Mitigations:* the Append ≡ Rewrite property in the default run, the
    delete/save/undo/save/reopen chain, `qpdf --check`, the journal, and
    stamp re-checks before every write.
- **False positives in the removal check** can force rewrites, or asks
  on signed files.
  - *Mitigations:* derived objects are judged on their inputs (exact);
    decoding goes through ToUnicode; subsets are GID-preserving; the
    corpus run counts vetoes per class.
  - *Remaining exposure:* vector geometry is out of scope, and an
    unknown encoding errs toward a Rewrite.
- **Value-validated commits plus `Relower`** are new concurrency
  machinery.
  - *Mitigations:* the commit rule is one function with a property test
    (a random Tx, a concurrent commit and an amend, then commit: the
    result is either a Conflict or a state containing both changes, and
    replay equals the snapshot), plus an explicit test for each policy
    row.
- **Live page tokens** rely on deterministic layout. A font or layout
  code change between sessions turns a reopen-and-reorder into a
  re-layout of every covered page. It still appends as `LIVE_REDRAW`,
  but it surprises.
  - *Mitigation:* the byte-determinism test and the planner's
    explanation. Proposal 8 lets Greg choose frozen tokens instead.
- **`keep_only` and the Rewrite path** still parse every reachable dict
  structurally, and 500 MB scans are unmeasured until slice 2.
  - *Mitigation:* slice 9's Rewrite gate. The port to a symbolic graph
    happens only if that gate fails.
- **The PageSpace switch** (slice 5) touches every gesture, drawing and
  hit-test site.
  - *Mitigations:* the `to_view ∘ from_view` property, and selftests on
    rotated, cropped pages.
- **Strict removal** sends more saves down the Rewrite path and asks
  more often on signed files.
  - *Mitigations:* raw passthrough keeps rewrites near I/O speed,
    "Append anyway" covers removals and form transforms, and the gate
    records Rewrite time.
- **The DocMDP, FieldMDP and UR3 mapping** rests on ISO 32000 and vendor
  docs.
  - *Mitigation:* warnings stay conservative until the Acrobat
    cross-check (Asks).
- **Interop is inferred.** Acrobat's handling of our named-property
  `/Artifact` blocks, and the `/PieceInfo` rule for its own, need an
  Acrobat round trip (slices 16 and 17).
- **Engine memory.** Each group engine parses its own xref and object
  cache over the same base.
  - *Mitigations:* LRU by measured bytes, a separate background budget,
    and geometry-only pages that need no overlay if spike C passes.
- **JSON ops over WIT** lose compile-time typing for non-Rust plugins.
  - *Mitigations:* `deny_unknown_fields`, per-kind refs with clear
    errors, the published schema and the `edit-schema` manifest check.
- **The `/SheafAnnot` v2 digest** must be computed identically on write
  and on read.
  - *Mitigations:* the canonical form, and a round-trip test for every
    mark kind, by both strategies and after a re-serialization by
    another tool.
- **Sources stay alive for the session.** Opening a huge file, deleting
  most of it and continuing keeps the whole file in memory until the
  document is closed.
  - *Accepted, and documented.*

## Asks

None of these blocks M10.5; each is wanted by the slice named.

- **Proposals 8–10, at kickoff:** live page numbers (slice 17), strict
  removal (slice 10) and exports that keep encryption (slice 19). The
  plan assumes the recommendations.
- **Signed and certified fixtures.** Slice 10 picks P1, P2 and P3
  certified and approval-signed fixtures by script from the 322
  `/DocMDP` files in the corpus. Greg's Acrobat check of "changes since
  signing" on them, and of the DocMDP, FieldMDP and UR3 mapping (§9.3),
  is wanted before M12, not before M10.5 ships.
- **Reference files from Acrobat:** a file with a header/footer and a
  watermark made by Acrobat, for the `/PieceInfo` rule (slices 6, 16 and
  17), and whether Acrobat can update or remove sheaf's.
- **Word-tagged PDFs** with running headers and page numbers (slices 6,
  16 and 17). LibreOffice ones can be generated if `soffice` is
  available.
- **Acrobat round trips, by hand** (recorded in the slice notes): open a
  sheaf file and edit our header and watermark; open signed files sheaf
  appended to and read "changes since signing".

## Not in M10.5

- **The semantic interpreter, glyph boxes, descriptors, removal
  transforms, a hard crop, the leak check:** M11.
- **The security dialog, new encryption, a permissions UI, the
  certificate refusal, `Key::Security`:** M11. (The serializer's
  encryption is here.)
- **Signing, reservations, byte-range holes, a diff per revision,
  `SigClass` SIGN and LTV, DSS:** M12.
- **Object editing, link bodies, key patches for annotations,
  `Source::users`:** M13. (The `KeyPatch` type lands in M10 slice 10.)
- **Object streams in written files, structure modeling, tagging copies,
  an embedded-font policy, PDF/A conversion, Save Optimized:** M14.
- **Batch steps, `BatesStart::Continue`, `PageSel::{Label, Matches}`:**
  M15.
- **The agent:** its transport, approval UI, `Actor::Agent` and plan
  handles are its own milestone.
- **`GroupKind::Typing`, `Actor::FormScript`, `PageSource::Template`,
  `FormBring::Flatten`, field ops, `FormDraw::Redacted`:** M10 slices
  11, 15, 19–22 and 29.
- **Joining an existing form instance on insert (`FormBring::Join`):**
  when a producer appears.
- **Three-way merges of ordered keys:** not planned; `Relower` covers
  it.
- **Persisted history, merge, OT or CRDTs:** not planned. Steps are
  serde-capable.
- **hayro as an engine:** not planned; an oracle only.
- **Shrinking `/SheafAnnot` to the keys PDF can't hold:** a follow-up.
- **The stamp library in the edit API:** not planned (proposal 29).
- **Linearization, and a balanced re-tree on Append:** later, if corpus
  tests ask for them.
